## Exact object and verified initial result (checkpoint 1161)

The actual family is
\[
 H_{t,2m}(z)=\int_0^\infty e^{tu^{2m}}\Phi(u)\cos(uz)\,du,
\]
using literally `RiemannQuarticKernel.kernel` and `RiemannQuarticFlow.halfLine`. Here m is a natural number, t is any real number and z any complex number; research priority is m>=2, with m=3 first. Algebraic validity at m=0 or m=1 does not open a separate quadratic campaign. No unverified identity with the completed Xi function is inserted as a hypothesis.

`RiemannEvenDomain.weighted_integrable` proves genuine integrability of every polynomial moment with every real even and linear tilt. `RiemannEvenFlow.jet_hasDerivAt` and `flow_differentiable` prove actual spatial differentiation and entireness by dominated local differentiation. `RiemannEvenEvolution.jet_time_hasDerivAt` proves actual real-time differentiation, using a local time majorant with tilt |t0|+1. `flow_iteratedDeriv` identifies every spatial derivative. `flow_eq_cosine_integral` proves the displayed integral identity. The exact m=2 agreement with the frozen quartic family is `RiemannEvenFlow.flow_quartic` (and `RiemannEvenDomain.weighted_quartic`), both by definitional equality.

The actual evolution theorem is
\[
 \partial_tH_{t,2m}=(-1)^m D_z^{2m}H_{t,2m};\qquad
 \partial_tH_{t,6}=-D_z^6H_{t,6}.
\]
These are Lean `HasDerivAt` theorems for genuine integrals, for every real t and complex z. They are not assertions about a merely formal exponential operator or positive-time stable stochastic law.

## Manuscript and literature audit

Its literal arithmetic kernel/positive quartic tilt occur at lines521-534. Preserved SHA256: `EAF31754F5F993EF8EB33A1917484D70F6B62F5B37A907AD85F097C3C5589D96`. The new actual PDE verifies the integer sign correspondence on its precise integral domain. The manuscript's generic polynomial nonpreservation claim does not decide eventual zeros of this particular kernel. No manuscript conjecture is accepted as a theorem, and no novelty claim is made.

Primary literature reviewed: Charles M. Newman, *Fourier Transforms with Only Real Zeros*, Proceedings of the AMS61(2),245-251 (1976), [primary paper](https://sites.math.northwestern.edu/~auffing/papers/Newman.pdf), Theorems1-3. Theorem1 contracts a zero strip under a positive Gaussian multiplier, assuming a real even integrable function, sufficient decay, and an initial zero strip. Theorem2 concerns all positive Gaussian dampings of an even nonnegative finite measure. These are quadratic statements; replacing the Gaussian exponent by u6 is not licensed by those theorems. Their hypotheses and multiplier directions must be checked before any application. OCR-garbled product formulas were not used as proof evidence.

NEW Foundation/RiemannEvenConcentration.lean upgrades qualitative shape to quantitative bounds on the ACTUAL amplitude a_{m,t}(v)=exp(tv^(2m))*Phi(v). Let n=2m, m>0, and T_m be the exact verified saddle-time map. The existing stationary curvature identity and scale bound imply T_m'(u)>3*T_m(u) on u>=n. Differentiating T_m(u)exp(-3u), with all continuity AND differentiability hypotheses discharged, proves T_m(v)>=T_m(u)exp(3(v-u))>=T_m(u)(1+3(v-u)) for n<=u<=v. No order-uniform time threshold is inferred from this explicit domain.

At an ACTUAL stationary center u>=n, T_m(u)=t>0. The exponential comparison gives S_{m,t}(v)<=-3*n*t*u^(n-1)*(v-u) for every v>=u. Applying derivative monotonicity to a(v)exp(c_R(v-u)^2), c_R=(3/2)n*t*u^(n-1), proves the GLOBAL REAL RIGHT Gaussian envelope
  a(v)<=a(u)*exp(-c_R*(v-u)^2), v>=u.
This bounds the literal kernel amplitude on the entire real right half-line, not a model saddle weight or a formal heat expectation.

On the left, the same comparison yields S_{m,t}(v)>=n*t*v^(n-1)*(u-v) when n<=v<=u and u-v<=1/2. A second explicitly differentiated integrating factor proves the corresponding actual left Gaussian bound with coefficient (1/2)n*t*v^(n-1). Combining sides gives a fixed half-unit TWO-SIDED amplitude window at any stationary u>=n+1:
  a(v)<=a(u)*exp(-c_L*(v-u)^2), |v-u|<=1/2,
  c_L=(1/2)n*t*(u-1/2)^(n-1).
The existing actual amplitude monotonicity then gives
  a(v)<=a(u)*exp(-c_L/4), n<=v<=u-1/2.
The interval begins at n; the compact interval[0,n] is NOT controlled by this far-left theorem. This window is in the INTEGRATION VARIABLE, not a real-rootedness or zero-localization window in the Fourier variable.

The actual_right_tail_integral_bound additionally proves, for every r>0,
  integral_{s>r} a(u+s) ds <= a(u)*exp(-c_R*r^2)/(2*c_R*r).
The proof first establishes actual shifted-amplitude continuity, measurability and integrability by the Gaussian majorant, then applies set-integral monotonicity and directly reuses RiemannQuarticGaussianTail.real_tail_bound. That declaration is a generic Gaussian c,r theorem despite its module name; no quartic-specific tilt is assumed. The integral is a genuine absolutely integrable actual-kernel tail, not a totalized undefined integral or numerical quadrature. For A6, n=6: c_R=9tu5 and c_L=3t(u-1/2)^5. A separate Lean file explicitly checks these constants and the actual tail-integral formula.

NEWLY CERTIFIED RiemannRealOrderBranchObstruction has7public theorems and no definitions/private helpers. no_analytic_even_odd_exponential proves a LOCAL obstruction: if natural n is ODD and real t!=0, no complex function analytic at0 agrees with exp(t*|x|^n) at all sufficiently near real x on BOTH sides. positive_half_line_density_extension gives the necessary qualification: exp(t*z^n)*kernelComplex(z) IS analytic at0 and agrees with the actual density for EVERY real x>=0, even when n is odd. Thus no claim of universal contour impossibility follows: a one-sided contour construction may remain possible; any endpoint, reflection or branch contribution needs its own proof.

New zero_time_density_extension proves the exact exception: at t=0, EVERY real-order density, pointwise on the whole real line, is exactly the existing actual kernel and admits its local analytic extension. Its statement is a zero-time density identity, not an integrability theorem for negative real orders. half_integer_fourier_entire_despite_density simultaneously proves, for odd n and nonzero t, the ACTUAL Fourier flow at order n/2 is entire and has positive value at frequency0, WHILE its even density has no analytic germ at the integration origin. It directly reuses the genuine real-axis integral construction. There is no contradiction: frequency holomorphy and analyticity in the integration variable are different requirements.

Scope limits are central. These are half-integer examples (e.g.3/2,5/2); they do NOT prove failure at every noninteger order, non-C-infinity regularity, a uniform obstruction arbitrarily close to1, failure of the constructed interpolation, impossibility of all contour methods, existence/location of actual nonreal zeros, or degeneration of a zero-localization region. [Csordas, Definition1.2](https://arxiv.org/html/1309.0055v2) requires smoothness, positivity, evenness, strict decrease and derivative decay for an admissible kernel. The current proof establishes nonanalyticity, which alone is not a proof that smoothness fails. Only the abstracts and targeted definition/theorem portions were read, not either entire paper.
